- **"กดวิเคราะห์แล้วต้องได้ผลลัพธ์ด้วยนะ ตอนนี้ยังไม่แสดงอะไรเลย"**
  - **Status**: IMPLEMENTATION
  - **Context**: The user wants the AI analysis results triggered by the `runAiBtn` in `sales-analysis.html` to appear in the UI. Currently, the button triggers the backend process, but the frontend lacks the logic to display the returned data or update the UI components (like the "AI Insights Summary Card") upon completion.

### 2. User Knowledge
- **Preferences/Decisions**:
  - The user explicitly requested an Excel report generation feature using `phpspreadsheet` (interpreted as Python equivalent `openpyxl`).
  - User feedback: "กดวิเคราะห์แล้วต้องได้ผลลัพธ์ด้วยนะ ตอนนี้ยังไม่แสดงอะไรเลย"
- **Credentials**: Groq API Key: `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` (Stored in `SystemSetting`).

### 3. Work Accomplished
- **AI Integration**: Fixed Groq API issues (re-validated key, updated model to `openai/gpt-oss-20b` as `llama3-70b-8192` was decommissioned).
- **Excel Export**: Refactored `/api/export/excel` in `main.py` to pull real `SalesData` from the database and use `openpyxl` for formatting.
- **UI Updates**:
  - Updated `overview.html`: Changed the "สร้างรายงาน" button link to point to the new `/api/export/excel` endpoint.
  - Backend: Fully functional Excel generation with dynamic column width formatting.

### 4. Model Knowledge
- **Architecture**: The application uses FastAPI (`main.py`) for the backend and HTML/JS (`sales-analysis.html`) for the frontend.
- **Constraint/Failures**:
  - The `runAiBtn` logic in `sales-analysis.html` (lines 545-573) fires a POST request but does not wait for a response or poll for status updates to refresh the UI with new insights.
  - The AI analysis currently runs as a `BackgroundTasks` process in FastAPI. The frontend needs a way to fetch the result once the background task finishes, possibly via a new GET endpoint for the latest insight or WebSocket updates.
  - Docker constraint: Running autogen requires a docker environment; current execution depends on `use_docker=False` or a properly configured environment.

### 5. Files and Code
- **Edited Files**:
  - `c:\laragon\www\spacial\backend\main.py`: Updated AI models (to `openai/gpt-oss-20b`), implemented real DB query for `/api/export/excel`, and fixed API key persistence.
  - `c:\laragon\www\spacial\overview.html`: Updated the "สร้างรายงาน" button to target the Excel export endpoint.
- **Viewed Files**:
  - `c:\laragon\www\spacial\sales-analysis.html`: Investigated `runAiBtn` click handler and existing UI visualizers (lines 131-250 for progress bars/summary card).

### 6. Current Work and Next Steps
- **Immediate Task**: Implement the display logic for AI analysis results. The user expects the "Summary Card" in `sales-analysis.html` to update after the analysis button is pressed.
- **Next Steps**:
  1. Create a new GET endpoint `/api/get-latest-insight` in `main.py` to retrieve the most recent `AiInsight` record.
  2. Modify the `runAiBtn` click event listener in `sales-analysis.html` to poll this new endpoint or wait for a signal that the task is complete.
  3. Update the innerHTML of the "AI Insights Summary Card" (lines 228-250) in `sales-analysis.html` with the data retrieved from the new endpoint.
  4. Verify if a WebSocket is needed for real-time status or if polling is sufficient for this MVP.

### 4. Model Knowledge

Scripts within these files must be re-initialized manually or wrapped in IIFEs (Immediately Invoked Function Expressions) because `DOMContentLoaded` only fires once on the initial page load.

### 4. Model Knowledge
- **Auth Flow**: `components.js` acts as a fetch interceptor. If a page's inline script runs before `components.js` completes initialization, `fetch` calls fail with `401 Unauthorized`.
- **SPA/DOM Lifecycle**: The custom SPA router (previously in `components.js`) was stripping scripts during partial page updates. Moving to standard browser navigation with carefully ordered script tags has resolved the `401` and "Loading..." staleness.
- **Encoding Constraints**: Windows (CP874) environments cause `UnicodeEncodeError` when printing certain characters; avoid logging non-ASCII output from AI models directly.

- **"สลับกล่อง ยอดขายรายสาขา กับ รายการสินค้า"** (Status: **NOT STARTED**) - Requested layout swap on the main dashboard to improve usability.
- **"ที่ขึ้นมันมองไม่เห็น มีอะไรบังมันอยู่"** (Status: **NOT STARTED**) - UI visibility issue; likely a CSS `z-index` or overflow conflict.
- **"นานมั้ยที่ผลลัพจะออกมา"** (Status: **NOT STARTED**) - Request for latency transparency/feedback regarding AI analysis.
- **"ตอนอยู่หน้าที่ 1 แล้วกดไปหน้าที่ 2 3 4 ยังปกติดี แต่พอย้อนกลับมาหน้าที่ 1 ดันต้องโหลดข้อมูลใหม่ทั้งหน้า"** (Status: **IMPLEMENTATION**) - User reports redundant re-loading when navigating back to the home/overview page via the SPA router.

### 2. User Knowledge
- **Preferences**: User prefers standard browser-based navigation but needs the SPA router to maintain state/performance without re-fetching everything.
- **Feedback**: "ตอนอยู่หน้าที่ 1 แล้วกดไปหน้าที่ 2 3 4 ยังปกติดี แต่พอย้อนกลับมาหน้าที่ 1 ดันต้องโหลดข้อมูลใหม่ทั้งหน้า"
- **Context**: User is sensitive to "Loading..." states and persistent page refreshing.

### 3. Work Accomplished
- **SPA Router Re-activation**: Re-enabled the SPA router in `components.js` to allow back/forward navigation without hard page refreshes, while keeping page-specific scripts encapsulated.
- **Cache Management**: Deployed `v=5` cache-busting to all `components.js` references.
- **Script Refactoring**: Moved `components.js` inclusion to the end of the `</body>` tag globally to ensure DOM elements are loaded before scripts execute. Used `fix_spa_components.py` to clean up redundant script tags and re-insert them correctly across all HTML files.
- **Auth Flow**: Maintained the central `fetch` interceptor in `components.js` for JWT-based API calls.

### 4. Model Knowledge
- **Architecture**: The application uses a PJAX-like SPA approach. The `components.js` file handles `click` events for internal navigation and intercepts them with `fetch` to swap out `<main>` tags.
- **Constraint**: Scripts injected via DOM replacement (SPA swap) do not auto-execute. They must be manually recreated/appended as elements to trigger correctly.
- **Failed Approach**: Attempted to move inline scripts inside the `<main>` tag using a regex-based Python script, which failed on complex files. Subsequently switched to a strategy of purely managing `components.js` and structure via a centralized fix script.
- **Performance**: API backend (`main.py`) is performant (~95ms), but UI loading overhead was causing the perceived delay.

### 5. Files and Code
- **Edited Files**:
  - `c:\laragon\www\spacial\components.js`: Re-enabled SPA router logic and fixed cache-busting versioning.
  - `c:\laragon\www\spacial\overview.html`, `sales-analysis.html`, `ai-insights.html`, `reports.html`, `data.html`, `ai-status.html`, `settings.html`: Cleaned up `components.js` references and ensured single inclusion at end of `</body>`.
- **Viewed Files**:
  - `c:\laragon\www\spacial\overview.html`: Key focus on lines 135-250 (Layout structure for Charts and AI Insights).
  - `c:\laragon\www\spacial\sales-analysis.html`: Reviewed for script insertion patterns.
